exports.buildLiveSequence = buildLiveSequence;
exports.buildLiveMassViewModel = buildLiveMassViewModel;
exports.buildMassMap = buildMassMap;
exports.recoveryTargets = recoveryTargets;
const mass_engine_1 = require(25);
const i18n_1 = require(17);
const liturgical_theme_1 = require(6);
function localized(text, language) {
    if (!text)
        return '';
    return (language === 'fr' ? text.fr : text.en) || '';
}
function latin(text) { return text?.lat || ''; }
function exceptionalProfile(value) {
    const map = {
        requiem_1962: 'requiem-1962', requiem: 'requiem-1962',
        holy_thursday_1962: 'holy-thursday-1962', holy_thursday: 'holy-thursday-1962',
        easter_vigil_1962: 'easter-vigil-1962', easter_vigil: 'easter-vigil-1962',
        palm_sunday_1962: 'palm-sunday-1962', palm_sunday: 'palm-sunday-1962',
        good_friday_1962: 'good-friday-1962', good_friday: 'good-friday-1962',
        candlemas_1962: 'candlemas-1962',
        ash_wednesday_1962: 'ash-wednesday-1962',
        ember_saturday_1962: 'ember-saturday-1962',
        extended_readings_mass_1962: 'extended-readings-mass-1962',
    };
    return value ? map[value] : undefined;
}
function activeMassSession(state) {
    const active = globalThis.AO_ACTIVE_MASS_SESSION;
    if (!active || active.date !== state.selectedDate || !active.resolvedMass?.canStart || !active.proper)
        return null;
    return active;
}
function specialSectionText(proper, id) {
    return (proper?.specialSections || []).find(section => section?.id === id)?.text || null;
}
function nuptialStep(id, title, subtitle, text) {
    return {
        id,
        phase: "Communion",
        section: "Nuptial Blessing",
        title,
        subtitle,
        actor: "celebrant",
        priestPosition: "Epistle side",
        audibility: "audible",
        privateAction: false,
        posture: { value: "CUSTOM", policy: "local", source: "nuptial-rite" },
        gestures: [],
        text,
        exceptional: { rite: "nuptial-mass-1962", phase: "Nuptial Blessing" },
    };
}
function insertNuptialRites(sequence, proper) {
    const first = specialSectionText(proper, "Benedictio Nuptialis 1");
    const second = specialSectionText(proper, "Benedictio Nuptialis 2");
    const finalBlessing = specialSectionText(proper, "Benedictio Finalis");
    let steps = sequence.steps.slice();
    if (first && second) {
        const pater = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "pater");
        if (pater >= 0) {
            const inserted = [
                nuptialStep("nuptial-blessing-1",
                    { lat:"Benedictio nuptialis", en:"Nuptial Blessing · First Prayer", fr:"Bénédiction nuptiale · Première oraison" },
                    { en:"After the Pater noster · the spouses kneel before the altar", fr:"Après le Pater noster · les époux s’agenouillent devant l’autel" }, first),
                nuptialStep("nuptial-blessing-2",
                    { lat:"Benedictio nuptialis", en:"Nuptial Blessing · Second Prayer", fr:"Bénédiction nuptiale · Deuxième oraison" },
                    { en:"The celebrant continues the Nuptial Blessing before Libera nos", fr:"Le célébrant poursuit la bénédiction nuptiale avant le Libera nos" }, second)
            ];
            steps = [...steps.slice(0, pater + 1), ...inserted, ...steps.slice(pater + 1)];
        }
    }
    if (finalBlessing) {
        const dismissal = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "dismissal");
        if (dismissal >= 0) {
            const finalStep = nuptialStep("nuptial-final-blessing",
                { lat:"Benedictio finalis sponsorum", en:"Final Blessing of the Spouses", fr:"Bénédiction finale des époux" },
                { en:"After the dismissal and before Placeat", fr:"Après le renvoi et avant le Placeat" }, finalBlessing);
            finalStep.phase = "Conclusion";
            finalStep.section = "Nuptial Blessing";
            steps = [...steps.slice(0, dismissal + 1), finalStep, ...steps.slice(dismissal + 1)];
        }
    }
    return { ...sequence, steps };
}
function supplementalCollectStep(kind, text) {
    const nuptial = kind === "nuptial";
    return {
        id: nuptial ? "nuptial-collect-commemoration" : "impeded-votive-collect-commemoration",
        phase: "Mass of the Catechumens",
        section: "Collect",
        title: nuptial
            ? { lat:"Oratio pro sponsis", en:"Nuptial Collect", fr:"Collecte nuptiale" }
            : { lat:"Oratio Missae votivae impeditae", en:"Collect of the impeded votive Mass", fr:"Collecte de la Messe votive empêchée" },
        subtitle: nuptial
            ? { en:"Added to the Collect of the Mass of the Day under one conclusion", fr:"Ajoutée à la Collecte de la Messe du jour sous une seule conclusion" }
            : { en:"Retained with the Collect of the Mass of the Day under one conclusion", fr:"Conservée avec la Collecte de la Messe du jour sous une seule conclusion" },
        actor: "celebrant",
        priestPosition: "Epistle side",
        audibility: "audible",
        privateAction: false,
        posture: { value: "STAND", policy: "rubric", source: "resolved-mass-assembly" },
        gestures: [],
        text,
        exceptional: { rite: "resolved-mass-addition", phase: nuptial ? "Nuptial Collect" : "Impeded Votive Collect" },
    };
}
function insertSupplementalCollect(sequence, requestedProper, kind) {
    const text = (requestedProper?.collects || [])[0] || requestedProper?.collect || null;
    if (!text)
        return sequence;
    const steps = sequence.steps.slice();
    const idx = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "collect");
    if (idx < 0)
        return sequence;
    return { ...sequence, steps: [...steps.slice(0, idx + 1), supplementalCollectStep(kind, text), ...steps.slice(idx + 1)] };
}
function applyResolvedAssembly(sequence, active) {
    const plan = active?.assemblyPlan;
    if (!plan)
        return sequence;
    const requestedProper = active.requestedProper || null;
    let out = sequence;
    if (plan.retainedCollect)
        out = insertSupplementalCollect(out, requestedProper, plan.kind === "nuptial-mass-of-day" ? "nuptial" : "votive");
    if (plan.nuptialBlessings)
        out = insertNuptialRites(out, requestedProper);
    return out;
}

function aoSelectedDateUtc(value) {
    const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(String(value || ''));
    if (!m) return null;
    return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3]), 12));
}
function aoGregorianEasterUtc(year) {
    const a=year%19,b=Math.floor(year/100),c=year%100,d=Math.floor(b/4),e=b%4,f=Math.floor((b+8)/25),g=Math.floor((b-f+1)/3),h=(19*a+b-d-g+15)%30,i=Math.floor(c/4),k=c%4,l=(32+2*e+2*i-h-k)%7,m=Math.floor((a+11*h+22*l)/451),month=Math.floor((h+l-7*m+114)/31),day=((h+l-7*m+114)%31)+1;
    return new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day, 12));
}
function aoSundayAspergesMode(state, inferredProfile) {
    if (state?.live?.form !== 'sung' || state?.settings?.sundayAsperges === false) return null;
    const date = aoSelectedDateUtc(state?.selectedDate);
    if (!date || date.getUTCDay() !== 0) return null;
    // Exceptional rites with their own pre-Mass ceremonial (especially Palm Sunday) do not receive this generic prepend.
    if (exceptionalProfile(inferredProfile)) return null;
    const easter = aoGregorianEasterUtc(date.getUTCFullYear());
    const pentecost = new Date(easter.getTime() + 49 * 86400000);
    return { vidiAquam: date >= easter && date <= pentecost };
}
function aoAspergesSteps(sequence, mode) {
    const chant = mode.vidiAquam ? AO_SUNDAY_ASPERGES_TEXT.vidiAquam : AO_SUNDAY_ASPERGES_TEXT.asperges;
    const introitSource = sequence.steps.find(step => step?.properSlot === 'introit' && step?.text)?.text || null;
    const introitEvent = {
        id: 'asperges-introit-start-choir', role: 'choir', properSlot: 'introit',
        properStatus: introitSource ? 'ready' : 'unavailable', continuation: true,
        ...(introitSource ? { text: introitSource } : {})
    };
    return [
        {
            id: 'asperges-chant', phase: 'Before Mass', section: 'Sunday Asperges',
            title: mode.vidiAquam ? {lat:'Vidi aquam',en:'Vidi aquam',fr:'Vidi aquam'} : {lat:'Aspérges me',en:'Asperges me',fr:'Asperges me'},
            subtitle: mode.vidiAquam ? {en:'Paschaltide · sprinkling before the principal Sunday Mass',fr:'Temps pascal · aspersion avant la Messe principale du dimanche'} : {en:'Sunday sprinkling before the principal Mass',fr:'Aspersion dominicale avant la Messe principale'},
            actor:'choir', priestPosition:'Altar and nave', audibility:'audible', privateAction:false,
            posture:{value:'STAND',policy:'rubric',source:'1962-sunday-asperges'}, gestures:[], text:chant,
            explanation: mode.vidiAquam ? 'The Schola sings Vidi aquam while the celebrant performs the Sunday sprinkling in Paschaltide.' : 'The Schola sings Asperges me while the celebrant performs the Sunday sprinkling before the principal Mass.'
        },
        {
            id:'asperges-versicles', phase:'Before Mass', section:'Sunday Asperges',
            title:{lat:'Versiculi et oratio',en:'Versicles & collect',fr:'Versets & oraison'},
            subtitle:{en:'The sprinkling concludes before Mass begins',fr:'L’aspersion s’achève avant le commencement de la Messe'},
            actor:'mixed', priestPosition:'Centre / foot of altar', audibility:'audible', privateAction:false,
            posture:{value:'STAND',policy:'rubric',source:'1962-sunday-asperges'}, gestures:[], text:AO_SUNDAY_ASPERGES_TEXT.versicles,
            explanation:'The Sunday sprinkling concludes with the appointed versicles and collect. The Mass itself has not yet begun.'
        },
        {
            id:'asperges-transition', phase:'Before Mass', section:'Transition to Mass',
            title:{lat:'Ad Missam',en:'Transition to Mass',fr:'Transition vers la Messe'},
            subtitle:{en:'After Amen: cope off · maniple and chasuble on · Introit begins',fr:'Après Amen : chape retirée · manipule et chasuble revêtus · l’Introït commence'},
            actor:'celebrant', priestPosition:'Sedilia / foot of altar', audibility:'quiet', privateAction:true,
            posture:{value:'STAND',policy:'rubric',source:'1962-sunday-asperges'}, gestures:[], choir:[introitEvent],
            explanation:'After the concluding Amen, the celebrant changes from the cope to the maniple and chasuble. This is the boundary at which the Schola begins the Introit; the Prayers at the Foot follow while the Introit continues.'
        }
    ];
}
function aoStripCrossRedundancy(sequence) {
    return { ...sequence, steps: sequence.steps.map(step => {
        const next = { ...step };
        if (next.id === 'sign-cross') next.title = { ...(next.title || {}), lat:'Introíbo', en:'Introibo', fr:'Introibo' };
        if (Array.isArray(next.gestures)) next.gestures = next.gestures.map(g => /^cross$/i.test(String(g?.kind || '')) ? { ...g, label:'' } : g);
        return next;
    }) };
}
function buildLiveSequence(state) {
    const resolution = state.resolution;
    const active = activeMassSession(state);
    const proper = active?.proper || (resolution?.proper.status === 'ready' ? resolution.proper.data : null);
    const path = active?.resolvedMass?.properSource || proper?.sourcePath || resolution?.day?.selectedMassOption?.path || resolution?.day?.main?.path || '';
    const bridgeProfile = active?.resolvedMass?.exceptionalProfile || (active?.resolvedMass?.celebrationType === 'requiem' ? 'requiem-1962' : null);
    const inferredProfile = bridgeProfile || proper?.riteProfile || (path ? (0, liturgical_theme_1.riteProfileFor)(path, { preparatoryLessons: proper?.preparatoryLessons || [], isRequiem: proper?.isRequiem }) : 'ordinary_mass');
    let sequence = (0, mass_engine_1.buildMassSequence)({
        form: state.live.form,
        proper,
        properAvailable: !!proper,
        faithfulCommunion: state.settings.faithfulCommunion,
        secondConfiteor: state.settings.secondConfiteor,
        joinConfiteor: state.settings.joinSecondConfiteor || state.settings.participationMode === 'group',
        incenseSung: true,
        exceptionalProfile: exceptionalProfile(inferredProfile),
    });
    if (active?.resolvedMass?.requestedCelebrationId === 'nuptial' && active?.resolvedMass?.celebrationId === 'nuptial')
        sequence = insertNuptialRites(sequence, proper);
    sequence = applyResolvedAssembly(sequence, active);
    const aspergesMode = aoSundayAspergesMode(state, inferredProfile);
    if (aspergesMode) sequence = { ...sequence, steps: [...aoAspergesSteps(sequence, aspergesMode), ...sequence.steps] };
    sequence = aoStripCrossRedundancy(sequence);
    return sequence;
}
function suggestedPostureValue(step, state) {
    const canonical=globalThis.AO_MASS_GUIDANCE_V4313?.postureFor?.(step?.id)||null;
    const action=String(canonical?.action||'').toUpperCase();
    if(action){
        if(action==='FOLLOW_LOCAL_CUSTOM') return null;
        if(['KNEEL','STAND','SIT','BOW','GENUFLECT','RISE'].includes(action)) return action;
    }
    /* Fail closed when the canonical guidance registry is present. */
    if(globalThis.AO_MASS_GUIDANCE_V4313) return null;
    const raw = String(step?.posture?.value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return raw && !/CUSTOM|LOCAL|FOLLOW/.test(raw) ? raw : null;
}
function postureLabel(step, language, state) {
    const saved = state?.settings.localPostures?.[step.id];
    const value = saved && saved !== 'follow' ? saved.toUpperCase() : suggestedPostureValue(step, state);
    if (!value) return null;
    const fr = { KNEEL: 'À genoux', STAND: 'Debout', SIT: 'Assis', BOW: 'Inclinaison', GENUFLECT: 'Génuflexion' };
    const en = { KNEEL: 'Kneel', STAND: 'Stand', SIT: 'Sit', BOW: 'Bow', GENUFLECT: 'Genuflect' };
    return language === 'fr' ? (fr[value] || value) : (en[value] || value);
}
function isChoirProjectedEvent(event, form) {
    return form === 'sung' && (event?.kind === 'choir' || /^choir$/i.test(String(event?.actor || '')));
}
function projectedTextKey(text) {
    if (!text) return '';
    return ['lat','en','fr'].map(k => String(text?.[k] || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()).join('||');
}
function dedupeProjectedEvents(events, form) {
    const out = [], byText = new Map();
    for (const original of events || []) {
        // A Low Mass has no Schola track even if a shared canonical step carries actor=choir.
        if (form !== 'sung' && original?.kind === 'choir') continue;
        const event = { ...original };
        if (event.kind === 'proper-unavailable') { out.push(event); continue; }
        const key = projectedTextKey(event.text);
        if (!key) { out.push(event); continue; }
        const prior = byText.get(key);
        if (!prior) { out.push(event); byText.set(key, event); continue; }
        const priorChoir = isChoirProjectedEvent(prior, form);
        const currentChoir = isChoirProjectedEvent(event, form);
        // Same text + same projected moment is one visual text. If one copy is the Schola
        // track, retain Schola identity so the surviving surface explains what is heard.
        if (currentChoir && !priorChoir) {
            prior.kind = 'choir';
            prior.actor = 'choir';
            prior.properSlot = event.properSlot || prior.properSlot;
            prior.continuation = !!event.continuation;
        }
        prior.mergedDuplicate = true;
    }
    return out;
}
function soundscape(step, events, language, form) {
    const choir = events.some(event => isChoirProjectedEvent(event, form));
    const altar = events.some(event => event.kind === 'altar' && !isChoirProjectedEvent(event, form));
    if (choir && !altar && ['quiet', 'silent'].includes(step.audibility))
        return (0, i18n_1.t)(language, 'choirContinues');
    if (!choir && !altar && ['quiet', 'silent'].includes(step.audibility))
        return (0, i18n_1.t)(language, 'sacredSilence');
    if (/consecration-(host|chalice)/.test(step.id))
        return (0, i18n_1.t)(language, 'sacredSilence');
    return null;
}
function isDualResponse(text) {
    const lat = text?.lat || '';
    const legacyAntiphonal = /(^|\n)\s*[SP]\.\s/m.test(lat) && /(^|\n)\s*M\.\s/m.test(lat);
    return !!lat && (/[℟℣]/.test(lat) || /\[ROLE=(MINISTERS|SERVER|PEOPLE|CONGREGATION|FAITHFUL)\]/.test(lat) || legacyAntiphonal);
}
function eventToBlock(event, step, language, textMode, form, compactContinuation = false) {
    if (event.kind === 'proper-unavailable')
        return { kind: 'unavailable', role: event.actor || '', primary: (0, i18n_1.t)(language, 'properUnavailableShort'), secondary: null, primaryLanguage: 'vernacular', dual: false, response: false };
    const text = event.text;
    const vern = localized(text, language);
    const lat = latin(text);
    const dual = isDualResponse(text);
    const choir = isChoirProjectedEvent(event, form);
    const latinFirst = choir || /consecration-(host|chalice)/.test(step.id);
    const meta = { kind: 'text', role: choir ? 'schola' : '', dual, response: dual, continuation: choir && !!event.continuation, compactContinuation: choir && !!compactContinuation, properSlot: event.properSlot || null, mergedDuplicate: !!event.mergedDuplicate };
    if (textMode === 'parallel')
        return { ...meta, primary: lat || vern, secondary: vern && vern !== lat ? vern : null, primaryLanguage: 'lat', dual: true };
    if (dual)
        return { ...meta, primary: lat || vern, secondary: vern && vern !== lat ? vern : null, primaryLanguage: 'lat' };
    if (latinFirst)
        return { ...meta, primary: lat || vern, secondary: vern && vern !== lat ? vern : null, primaryLanguage: 'lat', dual: false, response: false };
    return { ...meta, role: '', primary: vern || lat, secondary: lat && lat !== vern ? lat : null, primaryLanguage: 'vernacular', dual: false, response: false };
}
function buildLiveMassViewModel(state) {
    const sequence = buildLiveSequence(state);
    const index = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.live.stepIndex, sequence.steps.length - 1));
    const step = sequence.steps[index];
    const rawEvents = (0, mass_engine_1.projectStep)(step, state.live.followMode).events;
    const events = dedupeProjectedEvents(rawEvents, state.live.form);
    const language = state.language;
    const priorContinuationKeys = new Set();
    for (let i = 0; i < index; i++) {
        const priorEvents = dedupeProjectedEvents((0, mass_engine_1.projectStep)(sequence.steps[i], state.live.followMode).events, state.live.form);
        priorEvents.forEach(event => { if (isChoirProjectedEvent(event, state.live.form) && event.continuation && event.text) priorContinuationKeys.add(projectedTextKey(event.text)); });
    }
    return {
        language, sequence, index, step,
        title: localized(step.title, language), subtitle: localized(step.subtitle, language),
        section: step.section, phase: step.phase,
        progress: sequence.steps.length ? ((index + 1) / sequence.steps.length) * 100 : 0,
        progressLabel: `${(0, i18n_1.t)(language, 'currentStep')} ${index + 1} ${(0, i18n_1.t)(language, 'of')} ${sequence.steps.length}`,
        posture: postureLabel(step, language, state), priestPosition: step.priestPosition || null,
        gestures: (step.gestures || []).map(g => /^cross$/i.test(String(g.kind || '')) ? 'cross' : (g.label || g.kind)).filter(Boolean),
        bell: step.bells?.length ? `${(0, i18n_1.t)(language, 'bell')}${step.bells[0].pattern ? ` · ${step.bells[0].pattern}` : ''}` : null,
        soundscape: soundscape(step, events, language, state.live.form),
        textBlocks: events.map(event => eventToBlock(event, step, language, state.live.textMode, state.live.form, !!event.continuation && priorContinuationKeys.has(projectedTextKey(event.text)))),
        properUnavailable: step.properStatus === 'unavailable' || events.some(event => event.kind === 'proper-unavailable'),
        participationHint: state.settings.participationMode === 'group' ? (step.participationHint || null) : null,
        postureSource: state.settings.localPostures?.[step.id] && state.settings.localPostures[step.id] !== 'follow' ? 'saved' : (step.posture?.policy === 'custom' || /LOCAL|CUSTOM/i.test(step.posture?.value || '') ? 'local' : step.posture ? 'fixed' : null),
        form: state.live.form, followMode: state.live.followMode, textMode: state.live.textMode,
    };
}
function buildMassMap(state) {
    const sequence = buildLiveSequence(state);
    const seen = new Set();
    const out = [];
    sequence.steps.forEach((step, index) => { const key = `${step.phase}::${step.section}`; if (!seen.has(key)) {
        seen.add(key);
        out.push({ label: step.section, index, phase: step.phase });
    } });
    return out;
}
function recoveryTargets(state) {
    const seq = buildLiveSequence(state).steps;
    const first = (predicate) => Math.max(0, seq.findIndex(predicate));
    return [
        { key: 'footAltar', index: first(s => /Foot|Preparation/.test(s.section)) },
        { key: 'readingsGospel', index: first(s => /Epistle|Gospel/.test(s.section) || ['epistle', 'gospel'].includes(s.id)) },
        { key: 'offertory', index: first(s => s.phase === 'Offertory') },
        { key: 'canonSilence', index: first(s => /Canon/.test(s.phase) || /Canon|Consecration/.test(s.section)) },
        { key: 'communion', index: first(s => s.phase === 'Communion') },
        { key: 'conclusion', index: first(s => s.phase === 'Conclusion') },
    ];
}

function properText(proper, slot, stepId) {
    if (!proper)
        return undefined;
    switch (slot) {
        case "introit": return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.introit);
        case "collects": return (0, ordinary_1.concatText3)([ordinary_1.fixedOrdinary.collectDialogue, ...normaliseList(proper.collects)]);

            if (!chants.length)
                return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.gradual);
            const exact = chants.find(ch => (ch.id || ch.kind || "").toLowerCase() === stepId.toLowerCase());
            const stepKind = stepId.toLowerCase();

            const byIndex = stepKind === "gradual" ? chants[0] : stepKind === "alleluia" ? chants.find(ch => /allel/i.test(ch.kind || "")) || chants[1] : undefined;
            return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)((exact || semantic || byIndex || chants[chants.length - 1])?.text);
        }

        case "gospel": return (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.gospel);
        case "offertory": return (0, ordinary_1.concatText3)([ordinary_1.fixedOrdinary.offertoryDialogue, (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.offertory)]);

function properSlotHasSource(proper, slot) {
    if (!proper)
        return false;
    switch (slot) {
        case "introit": return hasText((0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper.introit));

function attachProper(step, proper, properAvailable) {
    const out = clone(step);
    if (out.id === "gospel-announcement") {
        out.text = gospelAnnouncementText(proper);
        if (Array.isArray(out.gestures) && out.gestures[0]) {
            out.gestures[0].anchorLat = 'Sequéntia ✠ sancti Evangélii';
            out.gestures[0].anchorEng = 'Continuation ✠ of the Holy Gospel';
        }
    }
    if (out.properSlot) {
        const sourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.properSlot);
        const resolved = sourceReady ? properText(proper, out.properSlot, out.id) : undefined;
        out.properStatus = hasText(resolved) ? "ready" : "unavailable";
        if (resolved)
            out.text = resolved;
        else
            delete out.text;
        if (out.id === "secret")
            out.audibleFragment = secretConclusion;
    }
    if (out.concurrentProperChoir) {
        const choirSourceReady = properAvailable && properSlotHasSource(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir);
        const choirText = choirSourceReady ? properChoirText(proper, out.concurrentProperChoir, out.id) : undefined;
        const dynamicEvent = {
            id: `${out.id}-${out.concurrentProperChoir}-choir`,
            role: "choir",
            properSlot: out.concurrentProperChoir,
            properStatus: hasText(choirText) ? "ready" : "unavailable",
            ...(choirText ? { text: choirText } : {}),
            continuation: true,
        };
        out.choir = [...(out.choir || []), dynamicEvent];
    }
    delete out.concurrentProperChoir;
    return out;
}

function filterOrdinarySteps(steps, options, proper) {
    let out = steps;
    if (proper?.hasGloria === false)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "gloria");
    if (proper?.hasCredo === false)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "credo");
    if (options.form === "sung" && options.incenseSung === false) {
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "incense-entrance" && step.id !== "incense-off");
    }
    if (options.omitJudica || proper?.isRequiem)
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "judica");
    if (options.faithfulCommunion === false) {
        out = out.filter(step => !["second-confiteor", "ecce", "domine-non-sum-dignus", "communion-faithful"].includes(step.id));
    }
    else if (options.secondConfiteor) {
        const idx = out.findIndex(step => step.id === "ecce");
        if (idx >= 0)
            out = [...out.slice(0, idx), makeSecondConfiteor(!!options.joinConfiteor), ...out.slice(idx)];
    }
    if (proper?.omitLastGospel || proper?.followedByLiturgicalAction) {
        out = out.filter(step => step.id !== "last-gospel-announcement" && step.id !== "last-gospel");
    }
    return out;
}
function insertSuperPopulum(steps, proper, properAvailable) {
    const text = properAvailable ? (0, ordinary_1.normaliseText3)(proper?.superPopulum) : undefined;
    if (!text)
        return steps;
    const idx = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === "postcommunion");
    if (idx < 0)
        return steps;
    const step = {
        id: "super-populum",
        phase: "Conclusion",
        section: "Postcommunion",
        title: { lat: "Oratio super populum", en: "Prayer over the People", fr: "Oraison sur le peuple" },
        actor: "celebrant",
        audibility: "audible",
        privateAction: false,
        text,
        properSlot: "superPopulum",
        properStatus: "ready",
    };
    return [...steps.slice(0, idx + 1), step, ...steps.slice(idx + 1)];
}
function buildMassSequence(options) {
    const proper = options.proper || null;
    const properAvailable = options.properAvailable !== false && !!proper;
    let steps = clone(options.form === "sung" ? sungTemplate : lowTemplate);
    steps = filterOrdinarySteps(steps, options, proper);
    steps = steps.map(step => attachProper(step, proper, properAvailable));
    steps = insertSuperPopulum(steps, proper, properAvailable);
    if (options.exceptionalProfile)
        steps = (0, exceptional_days_1.applyExceptionalProfile)(steps, options.exceptionalProfile, options.form, proper);
    return {
        form: options.form,
        properName: proper?.name,
        properStatus: properAvailable ? "ready" : "unavailable",
        steps,
    };
}
function properUnavailableText() {
    return clone(PROPER_UNAVAILABLE_TITLE);
}

function concatText3(parts, separator = "\n\n") {

function validateOrdinaryCorpora() {
    const issues = [];

            if (!value || typeof value !== "object") {
                issues.push({ corpus: name, key, issue: "Entry is not an object" });
                continue;

function stripLausTibi(text) {
    if (!text)
        return text;
    const out = clone(text);
    if (out.lat)
        out.lat = out.lat.replace(/\n*℟\.\s*Laus tibi,?\s*Christe\.?/gi, '');
    if (out.en)
        out.en = out.en.replace(/\n*℟\.\s*(Praise be to Thee|Glory to thee),?\s*O Christ\.?/gi, '');
    if (out.fr)
        out.fr = out.fr.replace(/\n*℟\.\s*(Louange|Gloire) à vous,?\s*ô Christ\.?/gi, '');
    return out;
}

function easterVigil(steps) {
    const donor = donorGroup('easter_vigil');
    const lead = donor.filter(step => ['vigil-fire', 'vigil-candle', 'vigil-lumen', 'vigil-exsultet', 'vigil-prophecies'].includes(step.id));
    const lauds = donor.find(step => step.id === 'vigil-lauds');
    let mass = trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'kyrie']);
    mass = mass.filter(step => !['introit', 'agnus', 'peace-prayer', 'communion-proper', 'last-gospel-announcement', 'last-gospel'].includes(step.id));
    const abl = mass.findIndex(step => step.id === 'ablutions');
    if (lauds && abl >= 0)
        mass = [...mass.slice(0, abl + 1), lauds, ...mass.slice(abl + 1)];
    return [...lead, ...clone(vigilBridge), ...mass];
}
function holyThursday(steps) {
    let out = steps.filter(step => !['judica', 'credo', 'peace-prayer', 'blessing', 'last-gospel-announcement', 'last-gospel'].includes(step.id));
    const dismissal = out.find(step => step.id === 'dismissal');
    if (dismissal) {
        dismissal.title = { lat: 'Benedicámus Dómino', en: 'Benedicamus Domino', fr: 'Benedicamus Domino' };
        dismissal.text = clone(benedicamus);
        dismissal.exceptional = { rite: 'holy-thursday-1962', phase: 'Mass ending', liturgicalColour: 'White' };
    }
    const placeat = out.findIndex(step => step.id === 'placeat');
    const tail = donorGroup('holy_thursday');
    if (placeat >= 0 && tail.length)
        out = [...out.slice(0, placeat + 1), ...tail, ...out.slice(placeat + 1)];
    return out;
}
function requiem(steps, form) {
    let out = steps.filter(step => !['incense-entrance', 'peace-prayer', 'blessing'].includes(step.id));
    const prep = out.find(step => step.id === 'gospel-prep');
    if (prep)
        prep.id = 'gospel-prep-requiem';
    if (form === 'sung') {
        const gospel = out.find(step => step.id === 'gospel');
        if (gospel) {
            gospel.text = stripLausTibi(gospel.text);
            gospel.variantNoteId = 'requiem-sung-no-laus-tibi';
        }
    }
    return out;
}
function applyExceptionalProfile(stepsInput, profileId, form, properInput) {
    const steps = clone(stepsInput);
    const proper = properInput;
    switch (profileId) {
        case 'candlemas-1962': return [...donorGroup('candlemas'), ...trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'introit'])];
        case 'ash-wednesday-1962': return [...donorGroup('ash_wednesday'), ...trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'introit'])];
        case 'palm-sunday-1962': return [...donorGroup('palm_sunday'), ...trimBefore(steps, ['incense-entrance', 'introit']).filter(step => !['last-gospel-announcement', 'last-gospel'].includes(step.id))];
        case 'holy-thursday-1962': return holyThursday(steps);
        case 'good-friday-1962': return goodFriday(proper);
        case 'easter-vigil-1962': return easterVigil(steps);
        case 'requiem-1962': return requiem(steps, form);
        case 'ember-saturday-1962':
        case 'extended-readings-mass-1962': return insertPreparatoryLessons(steps, proper);
    }
}

Hæc nox est, quæ hódie per univérsum mundum in Christo credéntes, a vítiis sǽculi et calígine peccatórum segregátos, reddit grátiæ, sóciat sanctitáti. Hæc nox est, in qua, destrúctis vínculis mortis, Christus ab ínferis victor ascéndit. Nihil enim nobis nasci prófuit, nisi rédimi profuísset.\n\nO mira circa nos tuæ pietátis dignátio! O inæstimábilis diléctio caritátis: ut servum redímeres, Fílium tradidísti! O certe necessárium Adæ peccátum, quod Christi morte delétum est! O felix culpa, quæ talem ac tantum méruit habére Redemptórem! O vere beáta nox, quæ sola méruit scire tempus et horam, in qua Christus ab ínferis resurréxit! Hæc nox est, de qua scriptum est: Et nox sicut dies illuminábitur: et nox illuminátio mea in delíciis meis.\n\nHuius ígitur sanctificátio noctis fugat scélera, culpas lavat: et reddit innocéntiam lapsis et mæstis lætítiam. Sed iam colúmnæ huius præcónia nóvimus, quam in honórem Dei rútilans ignis accéndit. Qui licet sit divísus in partes, mutuáti tamen lúminis detriménta non novit. Ille, qui regréssus ab ínferis, humáno géneri serénus illúxit.\n\nPrecámur ergo te, Dómine: ut nos fámulos tuos, omnémque clerum, et devotíssimum pópulum: una cum beatíssimo Papa nostro N., et Antístite nostro N., quiéte témporum concéssa, in his paschálibus gáudiis, assídua protectióne régere, gubernáre et conserváre dignéris. Réspice étiam ad eos, qui nos in potestáte regunt, et, ineffábili pietátis et misericórdiæ tuæ múnere, dírige cogitatiónes eórum ad iustítiam et pacem, ut de terréna operositáte ad cæléstem pátriam pervéniant cum omni pópulo tuo.\n\nPer eúndem Dóminum nostrum Iesum Christum, Fílium tuum: qui tecum vivit et regnat in unitáte Spíritus Sancti Deus: per ómnia sǽcula sæculórum.\n℟. Amen.","en":"Let the angelic host of heaven now rejoice; let the divine mysteries exult; and let the trumpet of salvation proclaim the victory of so great a King. Let the earth also rejoice, radiant with such splendour, and, enlightened by the brightness of the eternal King, know that the darkness of the whole world has been dispelled. Let Mother Church rejoice, adorned with the brightness of so great a light, and let this sacred place resound with the mighty voices of the people.\n\nTherefore, dearly beloved brethren, standing in the wondrous brightness of this holy light, I ask you to invoke with me the mercy of almighty God: that He who, without merit of mine, has deigned to number me among His ministers may pour into me the splendour of His light and enable me to complete the praise of this candle. Through our Lord Jesus Christ, His Son, Who with Him lives and reigns in the unity of the Holy Ghost, God, for ever and ever.\n℟. Amen.\n\n℣. The Lord be with you.\n℟. And with thy spirit.\n℣. Lift up your hearts.\n℟. We have lifted them up to the Lord.\n℣. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\n℟. It is meet and just.\n\nIt is truly meet and just to proclaim, with the whole affection of heart and mind and with the service of our voice, the invisible God, the almighty Father, and His only-begotten Son, our Lord Jesus Christ. He paid on our behalf to the eternal Father the debt of Adam, and by the merciful shedding of His Blood cancelled the bond of the ancient sin. For these are the Paschal feasts in which that true Lamb is slain, by Whose Blood the doorposts of the faithful are consecrated.\n\nThis is the night in which Thou didst first lead our fathers, the children of Israel, out of Egypt and make them pass dry-shod through the Red Sea. This is the night which dispelled the darkness of sin by the light of the pillar. This is the night which today, throughout the whole world, restores to grace and joins to holiness those who believe in Christ, separated from the vices of the world and the darkness of sin. This is the night in which Christ, breaking the bonds of death, rose victorious from the grave. For our birth would have profited us nothing had we not been redeemed.\n\nO wonderful condescension of Thy mercy toward us! O immeasurable love of charity: to redeem a servant, Thou gavest up Thy Son! O truly necessary sin of Adam, destroyed by the death of Christ! O happy fault, which merited to have such and so great a Redeemer! O truly blessed night, which alone deserved to know the time and hour when Christ rose from the grave! This is the night of which it is written: And the night shall be as bright as the day; and the night is my light in my delight.\n\nThe sanctification of this night therefore drives away wickedness, washes away guilt, restores innocence to the fallen and gladness to the sorrowful. It banishes hatred, prepares concord and bows down earthly pride.\n\nIn the grace of this night, then, receive, O holy Father, the evening sacrifice of this incense, which Holy Church offers to Thee in the solemn offering of this candle, the work of bees, through the hands of her ministers. We know now the praise of this pillar which the shining fire kindles in honour of God. Though its light is divided among many flames, it suffers no loss by being shared, for it is fed by the melting wax which the mother bee produced for the substance of this precious lamp.\n\nO truly blessed night, which despoiled the Egyptians and enriched the Hebrews! A night in which heavenly things are joined to earthly, and divine things to human.\n\nWe beseech Thee, therefore, O Lord, that this candle, consecrated to the honour of Thy Name, may continue unfailing to dispel the darkness of this night. Accepted as a sweet fragrance, may it mingle with the lights of heaven. May the morning star find its flame still burning: that Star, we mean, which knows no setting, Christ, Who returning from the grave shone serenely upon mankind.\n\nWe beseech Thee, therefore, O Lord, that Thou wouldst deign, in the joy of this Paschal feast, to rule, govern and preserve with Thy continual protection us Thy servants, all the clergy and Thy devout people, together with our most holy Pope N. and our Bishop N., granting us peaceful times. },35:function(module,exports,require){
"use strict";
Object.defineProperty(exports, "__esModule", { value: true });
exports.projectStep = projectStep;
exports.projectSequence = projectSequence;
function unavailable(step, slot, actor) {
    return {
        stepId: step.id,
        kind: "proper-unavailable",
        actor,
        properSlot: slot,
        note: "Proper unavailable; the fixed Ordinary remains available.",
    };
}
function choirEvents(step) {
    return (step.choir || []).map(event => {
        if (event.properStatus === "unavailable" || !event.text)
            return unavailable(step, String(event.properSlot || "proper"), "choir");
        return {
            stepId: step.id,
            kind: "choir",
            actor: "choir",
            text: event.text,
            properSlot: event.properSlot,
            continuation: !!event.continuation,
        };
    });
}
function projectStep(step, mode) {
    const events = [];
    const choir = choirEvents(step);
    if (mode === "missal") {
        if (step.properStatus === "unavailable" && step.properSlot)
            events.push(unavailable(step, step.properSlot, step.actor));
        else if (step.text)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
        events.push(...choir);
        return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
    }
    // VOX: choir is always part of what is actually heard.
    events.push(...choir);
    if (step.properStatus === "unavailable" && step.properSlot && step.audibility !== "quiet" && step.audibility !== "silent") {
        events.push(unavailable(step, step.properSlot, step.actor));
        return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
    }
    if (step.audibility === "audible") {
        if (step.text)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
    }
    else if (step.audibility === "mixed") {
        if (step.audibleFragment)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.audibleFragment, properSlot: step.properSlot });
        else if (step.text && !step.privateAction)
            events.push({ stepId: step.id, kind: "altar", actor: step.actor, text: step.text, properSlot: step.properSlot });
    }
    else if (step.audibility === "choir") {
        // Choir already emitted above.
    }
    return { mode, stepId: step.id, events };
}
function projectSequence(steps, mode) {
    return steps.map(step => projectStep(step, mode));
}

"use strict";
Object.defineProperty(exports, "__esModule", { value: true });
exports.validateMassStep = validateMassStep;
exports.validateMassSequence = validateMassSequence;
const VALID_AUDIBILITY = new Set(["audible", "quiet", "silent", "mixed", "choir"]);
function validateText3(value, path, issues) {
    if (!value)
        return;
    for (const lang of ["lat", "en", "fr"]) {
        const text = value[lang];
        if (text !== undefined && typeof text !== "string")
            issues.push({ path: `${path}.${lang}`, issue: "Must be a string" });
        if (typeof text === "string" && /<\/?[a-z][^>]*>/i.test(text))
            issues.push({ path: `${path}.${lang}`, issue: "HTML markup is not allowed in canonical liturgical data" });
    }
}
function validateMassStep(step, index = 0) {
    const issues = [];
    const path = `steps[${index}]`;
    if (!step || typeof step !== "object")
        return [{ path, issue: "Step must be an object" }];
    if (!step.id || typeof step.id !== "string")
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.id`, issue: "Stable id is required" });
    if (!step.phase || typeof step.phase !== "string")
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.phase`, issue: "Phase is required" });
    if (!step.section || typeof step.section !== "string")
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.section`, issue: "Section is required" });
    if (!step.title || typeof step.title !== "object")
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.title`, issue: "Title object is required" });
    validateText3(step.title, `${path}.title`, issues);
    validateText3(step.subtitle, `${path}.subtitle`, issues);
    validateText3(step.text, `${path}.text`, issues);
    validateText3(step.audibleFragment, `${path}.audibleFragment`, issues);
    if (!VALID_AUDIBILITY.has(step.audibility))
        issues.push({ path: `${path}.audibility`, issue: `Unsupported audibility: ${String(step.audibility)}` });
    if (step.choir) {
        step.choir.forEach((event, eventIndex) => {
            if (!event.id)
                issues.push({ path: `${path}.choir[${eventIndex}].id`, issue: "Chant event id is required" });
            validateText3(event.text, `${path}.choir[${eventIndex}].text`, issues);
        });
    }
    return issues;
}
function validateMassSequence(sequence) {
    const issues = [];
    const ids = new Set();
    sequence.steps.forEach((step, index) => {
        issues.push(...validateMassStep(step, index));
        if (ids.has(step.id))
            issues.push({ path: `steps[${index}].id`, issue: `Duplicate step id: ${step.id}` });
        ids.add(step.id);
    });
    return issues;
}
